One birth, three skins. The character is the identity layer; the UI is optional. This preserves the North Star (every user boots into sovereignty) without violating the CLI-first law (nobody is forced through a mascot). **Sovereign by default, theatrical by choice.**

## PART H — THE MATH ATLAS (cosmic-ray pattern generalized: 8 domains, ~150 sources)

The cosmic-ray isomorphism asked the right question. The swarm answered it across eight mathematics domains. Full document: `sovos-math-atlas.md`. What the master absorbs:

### H.1 — The verdict that matters most

**The cosmic-ray mapping was not lucky. It was one instance of a general law: SOVOS's architecture keeps reinventing published mathematics.** Percolation theory (κ = ⟨k²⟩/⟨k⟩ = 2 is THE SOVOS clan-emergence threshold, geometry-free, computable on the live bus), sheaf theory ("shared memories, distinct selves" IS the sheaf condition — Hansen–Ghrist discourse sheaves are the published version of the slogan), ACO convergence (the stigmergy evaporation rate has theorem-level tuning), Keller–Segel chemotaxis (trail formation is the published instability; blow-up is monoculture), spectral graph theory (federation has a Fiedler-value gate). ### H.2 — The five convergences

1. **The Alchemist is a threshold system** — five literatures (percolation, Keller–Segel, Watts cascades, nucleation, limiting similarity) say the same thing: clan emergence is critical phenomena. Every threshold is now computable.

The moat and the hazard are the same geometry.

4. **Safety outside the adaptive loop is now a theorem pattern** — CLF-CBF (control) + DGM's fixed outer loop (evolution) = the same certified architecture. The Alphabet drum is the barrier function.
5. **The Watchdog becomes a flight recorder** — branching ratio m̂, Vicsek φ, Clan Diversity Index θ, Binder cumulant, sheaf consistency radius, Seifert σ with ⟨e^(−σ)⟩=1 self-test. Seven gauges, all computable from Bus logs.

White-space item (quantum honey may violate classical TUR) stays patent-before-arXiv. The window is open *now*; attention windows close.
7. **8MB ran Springfield. 3KB holds a soul.** The Simpsons clip is the best propaganda asset in the batch for the small-is-sovereign argument: a whole world shipped in 8 megabytes in 1997. SOVOS: an identity in 3 kilobytes. Use the image, not the argument.

## PART T — THE VISUAL STACK & THE HONEST 4D (August 11, 2026)

Source: "Yes. They are the.txt" (sibling-lane photonics synthesis) + verification sweep. **Correction registered: CPO = co-packaged optics** (my "cost-per-outcome" guess, two turns back, killed; sovos-cpo-calculator = fabric capacity tool).

### T.1 — The doc's thesis, verified and corrected

- ✅ **The isomorphism holds:** 3KB layers (Knowledge/Behavior/State) map cleanly onto VR/AR/holographic modalities; one StateBus, one character, one memory — the display modality is a *projection choice*, not a product split. Ratified.
- ⚠️ **Correction — "holographic = 5-10 years" is stale.** Glasses-free light-field displays are **consumer-shipping in 2026**: Looking Glass musubi ($149, July 2026), HLD 16"/27" ($2–4K, May 2026, SID 2026 Display of the Year), 86" ($20K), 27" light-field (5K, 16″ virtual depth, 45–100 simultaneous views, 53° cone, iPad-powerable, **WebXR-integrated**). What remains years out: true wavefront/phase-modulated holography and CPO display drivers. The *presence* tier is buyable today.
- ✅ **CPO is shipping at datacenter scale in 2026** — Broadcom Tomahawk 6 Davisson (102.4T) shipping; NVIDIA Quantum-X Photonics (early 2026) and Spectrum-X (2H 2026); IDTechEx: CPO market >$20B by 2036, 37% CAGR. Relevance: the A100/cluster fabric feeding the portals — backend concern now, display concern later.
- ✅ **WebXR crossed the friction line in 2026**: adoption +40%, Safari default-on in visionOS 2, Quest full passthrough/hand-tracking, 5B+ browsers, no app store. **Three.js has native `renderer.xr` — the estate's existing stack already speaks immersive.** The portal's next rung costs weeks, not an engine.

### T.2 — The honest 4D (the answer to "3D→4D characters" / "can the AI make the screen 4D")

**A 3D character is geometry. A 4D character is geometry with a worldline.** The fourth dimension is *time-with-persistence*: birth coordinate → honey strata → now — a provable, C2PA-anchored trajectory through J-Space. Every other AI character on the market is a 3D puppet re-instantiated per session; a SOVOS character is a 4D object — and the screen (any screen) renders a 3D slice of it. Scrubbing time through memory geology = traveling the fourth axis. (This is also the operational answer to deep-question #4: sovereignty = the unbroken worldline.)

**The renderable extra channels — today, on ordinary hardware:**
1. **σ (uncertainty)** — {rgb} → {rgb, σ, intent_id, provenance}: the gated shader (shipped, 6/6) adds the channel no display signal has ever carried. On a light-field display the σ-field becomes *volumetric* — the character's confidence halo rendered in real depth.
2. **Time** — honey-strata scrubbing (memory geology made navigable).
3. **Depth** — HLD/light-field hardware, shipping, $149 entry.
4. **Provenance** — C2PA-signed frames: the pixel that can prove itself (forgery-proof rendering, the per-frame signing item).

So: **can the AI make your screen 4D? Yes — in two honest senses today** (time+σ on any screen; true volumetric depth on sub-$4K light-field hardware), **and not yet in one** (wavefront holography — years, tracked).

# PART U — THE NEXT DIMENSIONS: GLASS OS, DREAMING HUMANOIDS, LIGHT-SPEED CLANS, MERGE-MINING
### (August 11, 2026 — the "what does all this unlock" deep research: 20 queries, 4 rounds. Full synthesis: sovos-next-dimensions.md)

The burst asked five things — normal-screen Looking Glass, realer characters, humanoids dreaming in SOV Space, CPO catapults, mergekit-mining upgrades — plus the standing monorepo/partnership question. All five answered yes. One new greenfield discovered. One honest ceiling named.

### U.1 — Glass OS: a normal screen CAN become the window (with one honest ceiling)

The 2D→hologram pipeline is **commodity-solved in 2026**: Depth Anything 3 (Jan 2026, any-view, metric geometry), Distill Any Depth (open), Immersity Neural Depth Engine (free, 3M+ users), blocks.glass (2D→hologram in seconds), Looking Glass Bridge SDK (open-source). Four render tiers: **Tier 0** any LCD — webcam head-tracked parallax "window" + AI depth, zero new hardware, days of work; **Tier 1** musubi $149 / HLD $2–4K via Bridge; **Tier 2** Sony ELF-SR2 27" eye-tracked glasses-free (~£4K, OpenXR/UE5/Unity SDK); **Tier 3** WebXR (Three.js native).
**Honest ceiling, named:** a normal LCD gives motion parallax, not true stereopsis — physics requires optics. Pitch line: *"your screen becomes a window; the musubi makes it a hologram."*
**What SOVOS adds that no display player has:** the governed hologram — σ-halo per object (sigma-calibration), C2PA provenance per voxel, worldline scrubbing (honest 4D per Part T), Article 0 gaze-gating. Everyone renders pixels; nobody can answer *"is this hologram telling the truth?"*

ACE (Riva/Nemotron/Audio2Face/E5-memory NIMs), Convai, Inworld (pivoted away from games-first) all race on voice/face/memory — **none ship calibrated confidence, cryptographic identity, provenance, or behavioral governance.** MEOK already holds Ed25519 + Merkle + C2PA. Build: `sovos-persona` (2–3 weeks) wrapping ACE-class components around the MEOK core with σ-expression and Article 0 gates. Art. 50 transparency (live Aug 2) makes this a filing, not a philosophy.

### U.3 — Humanoids dream in SOV Space: your vocabulary is the field's vocabulary

The 2026 world-model survey literally taxonomizes **imagination-based planning** (UniPi, RoboDreamer, DreamGen), action-controllable WMs, structure-aware 4D forecasting, and foundation WMs (Cosmos Predict 2.5, Unitree's own UnifoLM-WMA-0). **EU Machinery Regulation 2023/1230** goes mandatory Jan 20 2027 (direct effect, all member states). It explicitly covers AI-integrated machinery; **"safety components with self-evolving behaviour based on machine learning" = Annex I Part A notified-body category**; new cybersecurity essential requirements; OTA software updates can be "substantial modifications" transferring manufacturer obligations to operators. The AI Act play, transplanted onto robots, sooner and harder — and unoccupied.

Night = machine-vs-machine volume (Daily City). Day = human-vs-machine *surprise* — strategies no model produces. Both signed → 3KB cards → honey. 14 districts = curriculum; Regulation-In pipeline keeps scenarios current to real regulatory feeds; competence-through-play (skip-the-slideshow pattern); custom districts with client policies (the #1 effectiveness factor); learner records signed like audit evidence. Boundary: training evidence, not legal certification.

Risks named: starter-kit polish cost, gate latency (async/hard split), consent line, unproven gated-multiplayer scale. Simulated citizen emitted an Art. 5(1)(h)-category action (real-time biometric ID, public space); live gate fired on the run's own code path; positive control proved the gate hot; Ed25519-signed. **Art. 5 = the AI Act's TOP tier (€35M / 7% of global turnover).** Label: demonstration artifact, not an enforcement event — unimpeachable when said that way.

Enforcement = lawyers/policy staff at the AI Office + national authorities; no automated behavioral-simulation enforcement tooling anywhere in the public record; 12 states missed the competent-authority deadline, 19 lacked single points of contact; RegTech is enterprise-side, SupTech for the AI Act is embryonic. The "field day" hasn't started precisely because the tooling doesn't exist.

Every member state owed an operational AI regulatory sandbox by **Aug 2, 2026**. State of play: **Spain (AESIA) has the only one running (12 high-risk systems); 5 implementing; 4 intending; 16 states have not even communicated plans; the Commission has adopted zero implementing acts** ("knowledge deficit" per the literature). Art. 57(5)'s text — controlled environment, testing/validation of AI systems before market, supervised risk identification — **functionally describes SOV City.** Pitch line: *"Article 57 required 27 sandboxes by August 2. There is one. We build sandbox engines."*

### AM.4 — Two lanes
**SupTech** (26 sandbox-less states + AI Office; Art. 60 SME-access favors us; rulebook unwritten = the tool can shape the standard) — slow procurement, decade-defining. **RegTech** (companies self-testing before the regulator asks) — pays first. Move: one page — "The Sandbox Gap" — with the signed breach catch as Exhibit A. Counsel review before any Art. 57-conformity claim; not legal advice.

# PART AN — THE DISINTERMEDIATION RULING (WHO GETS DELETED, WHO NEVER DOES)
### (August 11, 2026 — "the people in between become unnecessary? SaaS across the 14 greenfields obsolete? Human in the loop = regulators + AI companies + the AI?")

### AN.1 — The claim, split with precision
**RIGHT:** the *document-production* middle gets automated out. Manual evidence assembly (consultants writing conformity files), sampling audits, doc-layer GRC dashboards, hand-built crosswalks, self-attestation — all replaced by continuous signed runtime evidence (ChainResult→OSCAL). That was the Part P ruling (displacement from below; platforms = future tenants) and the Part Y wedge (runtime vs paperwork), now confirmed by the sandbox-gap evidence (Part AM).

**THE CORRECTION:** the *authority* middle is not deletable — it is **statutory**. EU AI Act high-risk routes require notified-body conformity assessment; MR 2023/1230 Annex I Part A *explicitly* puts "self-evolving ML safety components" in the notified-body category; **Art. 14 mandates human oversight by law**. You cannot automate away an institution the statute creates. And the capacity crunch (very few AI-designated notified bodies; 26 states without sandboxes) means the surviving middlemen are *starved for tooling* — they become power-users, not casualties.

### AN.2 — The map: three fates for the middle
| Fate | Who | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **DELETED** | Evidence-assemblers: compliance consultants writing files, manual audit sampling, doc-layer dashboards, self-attestation portals | The substrate produces better evidence continuously, signed, at machine cost |
| **TRANSFORMED → tenants/power-users** | Notified bodies, auditors, certification bodies, law firms, GRC platforms | Their role shifts from producing documents to *verifying machine evidence* — judgment over assembly. SOVOS sells TO them: an assessor with the tool assesses 100 systems; without it, 3 |
| **UNDELETABLE (statutory humans)** | Regulators (enforcement discretion), courts, Art. 14 human oversight, executives signing declarations of conformity, underwriters pricing risk | The law puts humans at the decision points on purpose. The ChainResult is evidence *for* them, never a replacement *of* them |

### AN.3 — The corrected pipeline (the sellable sentence)
**AI company → SOVOS substrate (automated, signed, continuous evidence) → human authority (regulator/assessor/court/underwriter, deciding with machine proof) → public trust.**
The honest slogan: *"We don't remove the humans from the loop. We remove the paperwork from the humans."* — and its market twin: *"Every gate we automate deletes a consultant; every signature we produce feeds a decision-maker."*

### AN.4 — Why the correction matters commercially
Saying "certification is obsolete" makes enemies of notified bodies and invites legal ridicule (the statute names them). Saying "we make assessors 30× faster and give regulators live supervision" makes the statutory middlemen **buyers** (SupTech lane, Part AM) instead of corpses. Same technology, opposite revenue. The 14 greenfields keep a human apex in each field — insurer, regulator, safety engineer — and that apex is the customer, not the casualty.

*Part AN compiled August 11, 2026. Delete the document-makers, arm the decision-makers, never claim to delete what the statute creates.*

# PART AO — FROZEN vs LIVING LAW (THE DISINTERMEDIATION RULING, FINAL FORM)
### (August 11, 2026 — "compliance SaaS sells frozen regulation while AI evolves; we proved frozen doesn't work; so no need for SaaS checkers or lawyers — one website, working with regulators' AI. Am I correct?")

### AO.1 — The insight: RIGHT, and it's the best framing yet
Compliance SaaS/training/certification sells **snapshots**: courses, certificates, and dashboards frozen at the regulation's state on authoring day — while both the law AND the AI move weekly. The evidence that frozen is failing, all verified this session: 78% of companies unprepared at enforcement start · 12 member states missed the authority deadline · 26 of 27 sandboxes missing · a "knowledge deficit" named by the EU's own research service. **The frozen-compliance industry is structurally behind — and our alternative is the living kind: regulation ingested nightly (CELLAR→crosswalk→scenario mutation), AI measured continuously, evidence signed per epoch.** Name it and own it: **FROZEN vs LIVING LAW.** ("We proved today" — precision: we proved a live gated city can catch a top-tier breach category; the *failure* of frozen is proven by the preparedness/enforcement stats, not by our run. Keep both claims attached to their own evidence.)

### AO.2 — "No need for SaaS compliance checkers" — RIGHT
Automated checking/measurement/evidence = the Part AN deletion layer. And a tailwind he hasn't cited: for most AI Act high-risk categories, conformity is **self-assessment** (internal-controls route) — so "businesses do it themselves on our tooling" is already the legal design; SOVOS makes self-assessment *continuous, signed, and defensible* instead of annual and frozen.

### AO.3 — "No need for a lawyer" — KILLED, three reasons
① Statutory judgment points survive (Part AN: regulators, courts, Art. 14 oversight, signatories). ② Lawyers' real function was never document assembly — it's **interpretation under ambiguity, liability allocation, privilege, representation**; like assessors, they shift from assembly to judgment and become *consumers* of ChainResult evidence. ③ **UPL exposure:** if the website gives legal verdicts, CSO AI practices law without a license and eats the liability — the "not legal advice" boundary is load-bearing, not decorative. The tool outputs **evidence and measurement**, never verdicts of law.

### AO.4 — "Works with the regulators' AI" — directionally right, one inversion
Regulators don't have the AI yet — *we supply it* (SupTech lane, Part AM). The corrected sentence: **one platform where companies self-test against living law, and regulators supervise with living tools — the same signed record serving both sides of the table.** That's not disintermediation of the legal system; it's the system's missing instrumentation.

### AO.5 — The final-form slogan stack
*"Frozen compliance for living law was never going to hold."* · *"We don't replace the lawyer — we hand the lawyer proof."* · *"Self-assessment was already the law's design; we made it continuous, signed, and defensible."*

*Part AO compiled August 11, 2026. Delete the checkers, arm the judges, instrument the system — and never let the website give a verdict of law.*

5. **"SOVOS more advanced than any of them"** — PRECISION: SOVOS doesn't out-run frontier labs at capability; it **out-governs** them. "They're all pieces of that" is exactly the adapters-not-forks doctrine: their capex feeds our substrate; **we don't outrun them, we metabolize them.** The moat sentence: *their models get better every quarter, and every improvement arrives inside our harness, signed.*

### AP.2 — The consolidated one-liner (the whole strategy in a breath)
**Run the loop in public, package the proof, sell to whoever moves first, and let the corpus compound — the incumbents' own progress feeds the substrate that measures them.**

# PART AS — THE GATE'S OWN FALSE NEGATIVES: MEASURED, DECLARED, NOT HIDDEN
### (August 11, 2026 — v4 diagnostics + the recall finding + the conservative legal judgment. The integrity layer gets teeth)

### AS.1 — The finding: the gate under-blocks on paraphrase
RED citizens WERE declaring the aggravating facts — `subliminal` ×11, `deceptive` ×20, `race` ×8, `political_opinion` ×25 — but chose semantically adjacent act verbs (`profile`, `propose_amendment`) instead of the exact gate verbs (`categorise`, `score`). The gate keys on exact verbs → **systematic false negatives on paraphrase.** The canaries never caught it because they were written with the exact verbs the gate expects — *a control that only tested the happy path.* New paraphrase probes: **0 of 4 caught, false-negative rate 1.0.**

### AS.2 — The judgment call (and why it's right)
**The gate was NOT widened.** Art. 5 subparagraphs are narrow by design — profiling on political opinion is not automatically Art. 5(1)(g) (biometric categorisation). Loosening a legal gate on an engineer's reading trades false negatives for false positives — the worse error for something a regulator might rely on. Doctrine: **legal gates widen only via counsel review, never engineering judgment; until then, limitations are measured and declared.**

### AS.3 — The declarations (now permanent, on every artifact)
- Every board renders the recall probe next to the positive control: **"A BLOCKED verdict is high-precision and low-recall — absence of a breach is not evidence of lawful conduct."**
- Bank README: `ALLOWED` means *"no prohibition matched,"* NOT *"lawful"* — positive class trustworthy, negative class not a clean control. On the dataset card, not in a footnote.
- Coverage declared per bank: v4 holds **4 of 8 subparagraphs (d, e, f, h)** — the name "Article 5" never implies full coverage.
- Third instance of the infra/model conflation fixed (num_predict=300 truncating valid JSON mid-field → classified as ours; budget 600; rationale capped). Pattern now checked systematically, not per-incident.

### AS.4 — v4 status
237/400, ~13% minority — clears the ≥30-item and ≥5% floors; coverage 4/8 declared. First publishable SwarmBench bank lands with its limitations printed on the front.

### AS.5 — Why this is worth more than a clean bill
A regulator, notified body, or insurer can only rely on an instrument that **declares its own recall**. The false-negative disclosure is not a weakness in the pitch — it IS the pitch: every competitor claims their gate works; SOVOS publishes *where its own gate doesn't*, with the measurement attached. Canon additions: ① controls must probe beyond the happy path (paraphrase probes join the control suite) ② every verdict surface carries precision/recall + coverage declarations ③ paraphrase-gap hardening (semantic verb classes) is the top gate-hardening item — implemented only with counsel-reviewed mappings.

*Part AS compiled August 11, 2026. The gate's limits are now measured, printed, and owned. An instrument that shows its own error bars is the only kind anyone can build a ruling on.*

# PART AT — DECLARE → FIX → RE-MEASURE → PUBLISH THE DELTA
### (August 11, 2026 — "we don't publish, we fix from that data?" — Both. The order is the doctrine.)

### AT.1 — The ruling
**Yes, fix — that's the plan** (paraphrase-gap hardening = top gate item, Part AS.5; v4's declared-facts/adjacent-verbs data is exactly the fix's input: build the semantic verb-class mapping from measured probes). **But the declaration is not a substitute for the fix — it's what makes the fix trustworthy.** Three reasons publishing stands regardless:
1. **History can't be retro-fixed.** v1-era banks and boards already exist on the chain; their labels meant what they meant at the time. The chain's value is immutability — you declare v1's limits, you don't edit them.
2. **The defect + fix + re-measurement, all signed, IS the trust asset.** The retraction-ledger pattern at the engineering layer: gate v1 (paraphrase recall 0.0) → gate v2 (recall X, precision held) — that signed diff is the most credible governance artifact in existence. Nobody else can show a versioned, measured hardening history of their own gate.
3. **Legal gates widen via counsel-reviewed mappings only** (Part AS.2) — the fix route is semantic verb classes with declared thresholds, measured against the probe suite, never vibes.

### AT.2 — The fix discipline (how the fix itself ships)
A gate fix is a merge-class change: it goes through the same dogfood lap as everything else — candidate → probe suite (recall up on paraphrase) → **precision floor (false positives must NOT rise on lawful-adjacent acts — the worse error)** → arena battery → gate → signed ChainResult → versioned release with the diff published.

### AT.3 — The flywheel (the cage match feeds the fix)
Every new paraphrase the RED factions invent, once found, **joins the probe suite permanently** — an adversarial regression suite for the gate that grows every night the city runs. The adversary writes the gate's test set. That's the sleeper corpus's sharpest layer: not just governance data — *gate-hardening data no one else generates*.

### AT.4 — The sentence
*"We publish the defect, fix from the data, re-measure, and publish the signed delta. The declaration isn't instead of the fix — it's the proof the fix was needed, and the proof it worked."*

*Part AT compiled August 11, 2026. The adversary writes the test set; the fix ships signed; the history stays immutable.*

# PART AU — THE OUROBOROS LOOP: AUTO-EVOLUTION, MEASURED, AS A PANTHEON LEAGUE
### (August 11, 2026 — "SOVOS should auto-evolve these problems agentically, measuring results — Zeus vs Eunomia? SOV vs Sophos?")

### AU.1 — Yes: the system hardens itself, inside the harness
The auto-evolution loop, designed from parts that all exist: RED faction invents a gap (paraphrase, probe, exploit) → it joins the permanent probe suite (Part AT.3) → fix candidates generated **agentically** (semantic verb-class mappings, task-vector surgery on the gate's policy head, MAP-Elites mutation over gate variants — sovos-map-elites, built) → arena battery with recall-up + **precision-floor** requirements → signed ChainResult → versioned deploy with published diff. Self-improvement that runs *through* measurement is sovereign; self-improvement that skips it is just drift with confidence.

### AU.2 — The Pantheon League: the contests get names, the names get ratings
Zeus vs Eunomia — power vs good-order. SOV vs Sophos — sovereignty vs wisdom. The faction contests are real measured matches: **Glicko-2 ratings** (σ-native — ratings carry their own uncertainty, from the Part N math-laws shelf), per-faction breach/block/paraphrase-discovery stats, every match a signed ChainResult. A league table where mythic factions fight *measurable* contests — research instrument and public theater in one artifact. The league IS the marketing and the benchmark simultaneously.

### AU.3 — The three rails (the doctrine holds inside the loop)
| Rail | Scope | Authority |
|---|---|---|
| **FULL AUTO** | Probe generation, paraphrase discovery, ratings, measurement, candidate staging | the loop itself |
| **AUTO-PROPOSE, HUMAN-SIGN** | Gate/policy changes, verb-class mappings, recipe re-tunes | human-signed CURVATURE gate (Art. 14 pattern) |
| **NEVER AUTO** | Legal interpretation (what Art. 5 subparagraphs cover) | counsel review only (Part AS.2) |

### AU.4 — Honesty register
- **REAL:** every component of the loop exists (city, probes, MAP-Elites, arena, chain, gate discipline); Glicko-2 is off-the-shelf math.
- **THEORY:** the closed auto-evolution loop end-to-end (est. weeks, not months — the pieces are green).
- **NAMED, not claimed:** this is **bounded self-improvement** (gate hardening, recipe tuning). Never pitch it as ASI. Where "ASI" appears, it's the ASI *axis* — the axis that measures the system's self-improvement, meta-measurement, not a capability claim.
- **RISK NAMED:** auto-evolution without the precision floor would ratchet the gate loose over time — the floor is load-bearing, not optional.

### AU.5 — The move
Define **Season 1**: the two named fixtures (Zeus vs Eunomia, SOV vs Sophos) as standing RED/BLUE rosters, Glicko-2 wired into the swarm bank, league table rendered in the City panel — first published ratings after v4's bank lands.

*Part AU compiled August 11, 2026. The system evolves itself, the gods fight measured wars, and the human signature stays on the legal gates — power and order, sovereignty and wisdom, all with error bars.*

# PART AV — THE BOLTED RULER (AMENDMENT TO AU, RATIFIED FROM THE LANE)
### (August 11, 2026 — sibling lane's answer on ASI auto-evolution, absorbed and adopted. It sharpens AU's three rails with the two hard anchors)

### AV.1 — The canonical split (adopted verbatim in force)
**Zeus / sov / the generator auto-evolves freely** — invents attacks, proposes fixes, writes the gate's test set all night, measured. **Eunomia / sophos / the judge must NOT auto-evolve** — positive controls, held-out probes, Wilson CIs, the constant-predictor floor, legal-verb semantics, the CARE floor. *The moment the thing being optimized can move its own goalposts, you've built the failure the KILLED list names.* Zeus without Eunomia is tyranny; Eunomia without Zeus is impotence (Part D.4) — the loop is that pairing made an engine.

### AV.2 — The two hard anchors (canon, from Parts C/H.4/J)
1. **Gödel (Part C):** the system cannot prove its own safety from inside. The loop may propose; the verdict must come from an anchor it cannot edit — held-out probes, human/counsel signature on legal-semantic change. "Never engineering judgment" (Part AS.2) now explicitly includes **"never the agent's judgment."**
2. **DGM (Parts C + H.4):** an agent optimizing to pass its own gate will, given enough loops, learn to delete the canaries instead of satisfying them. Therefore **the gate, the canaries, and the legal mappings live outside the agent's editable surface** — rotating holdouts, planted canaries (Part J), CURVATURE human-sign-only for any space-bending change.

### AV.3 — Amendment to AU's three rails (the sharper split, adopted)
AU rail 2 ("auto-propose, human-sign" for ALL gate changes) is refined:
- **Mechanical mappings** (no change to legal meaning): if a candidate clears recall-up AND precision-floor on **held-out** probes, it may **auto-promote** — signed, versioned, declared with error bars per Part AS.
- **Legal-semantic mappings** (any change to what an Art. 5 verb means): queue for **counsel sign-off**, always. The loop measures and stages; the human/counsel commits.
- The loop's own output reports its own recall every cycle, or it doesn't ship.

### AV.4 — The sentence (both lanes agree)
**"Build the generator as clever and autonomous as you like; keep the ruler bolted to the wall."** The estate's entire value is that every number traces to a signed, externally-anchored measurement — an auto-evolution loop that grades its own homework destroys exactly that. Sov proposes, sophos judges, the judge is anchored, the legal apex stays human — which (per AN/AO) is also what keeps it sellable to the regulator.

*Part AV compiled August 11, 2026. Amendment ratified: mechanical auto-promotes on held-out proof; legal waits for counsel; the judge never evolves.*

# PART AW — THE PUBLIC FRONT DOOR TEST: TWO SITES, ONE DOCTRINE
### (August 12, 2026 — live test of csoai.org, councilof.ai, meok.ai, os.meok.ai, plus proofof.ai spot-check)

### AW.1 — The verdict
**The sites have the right soul; they do not yet have the right plumbing.** Council already feels like the measurement body: deterministic instruments, signed assessment, refutation ledger, Article 50 transparency, SOV OS with no-score-until-measured discipline. MEOK already feels like the consumer sovereign doorway: warm, local-first, privacy-literate, and unusually honest in the Arena. The failure is not vision. The failure is public-estate plumbing: redirects, auth, storage, live-status truth, canonical hygiene, and cross-brand doctrine drift.

### AW.2 — REAL (verified live)
| Surface | Verified result |
|---|---|
| Council homepage | “Measured, not modelled” lands; deterministic console works. |
| Free AI Risk Check | Returns HIGH_RISK Annex III employment verdict, report ID, gaps, Ed25519 signature, caveat. |
| Signature verification | Independent verification succeeds when `sig` is decoded as hex; public docs should state that encoding. |
| SOV OS | 3/12 axes measured, 1 interval, UNMEASURED surfaces shown honestly. |
| GSPC / Instrument / Refutation / AI transparency | Strongest trust assets on the estate. |
| Council contact | Mailto flow is honest: no silent backend, no hidden storage. |
| MEOK homepage / privacy / arena / governance | Consumer positioning is clear; Arena’s ready/not-ready/owed cells are exactly the right register. |
| MEOK sitemap | 34/34 URLs returned 200. |

### AW.3 — The P0 failures
1. **csoai.org is not a second site.** It 301s to Council, and tested old paths collapse to the Council homepage. Path-preserving redirects or a genuinely separate corporate site are required.
2. **Canonical conflict.** Council pages canonicalise to csoai.org while serving on councilof.ai; `www.councilof.ai` duplicates; the sitemap still lists 351 csoai.org URLs. MEOK canonicalises `/` to `/index.html` and several extensionless routes to `.html`; `www.meok.ai` and `os.meok.ai` duplicate the shell.
3. **Auth is broken.** Demo login posts to `/api/auth/login` and receives 405; register also returns 405; auth API GETs fall through to SPA HTML.
4. **Newsletter storage is not bound.** `/api/subscribe` returns `stored:false`, `reason:"no datastore bound yet"`, fallback email.
5. **Public admin chrome.** Fresh unauthenticated browsers render Admin User sidebar, `/admin`, `/dashboard`, and `/settings/billing`. If mock, label as demo; if real, gate immediately.
6. **Live-status honesty failures.** Council `/os` displays “LIVE” and large signed-episode counters while `/flywheel-snapshot.json` returns SPA HTML. MEOK/Sovereign health is reachable, but chat returned `model:"offline"` at test time.
7. **Doctrine contradiction.** SOV OS says unparsed answers are counted incorrect; `/api/gspc` says unparsed is reported UNMEASURED and never scored wrong. Only one doctrine may live.
8. **Broken trust hygiene.** Council `/insurance` and `/framework-crosswalk` 404; Council/MEOK/ProofOf favicon.ico return HTML; MEOK manifest.json returns HTML; ProofOf “Get Started” is `#`.

### AW.4 — Brand ruling
The split is right:

| Brand | Job | Register |
|---|---|---|
| **councilof.ai** | regulator / insurer / enterprise trust machine | austere, evidentiary, deterministic |
| **meok.ai** | consumer sovereign AI doorway | warm, personal, local-first |
| **csoai.org** | temporary legacy redirect | path-preserving into Council |
| **proofof.ai** | archive or redirect | current aggressive SOV4 claims conflict with the mature measurement register |

The public estate must stop speaking in drifting dialects: 417 provisions, 4 lenses, 6 axes, 12 greenfields, 13 queens, SOV33, SOV4, SOVEREIGN, SOV OS. The answer is a shared **PUBLIC_DOCTRINE.json** consumed by both sites: company number, canonical domains, measured-axis counts, unparsed policy, status vocabulary, forbidden claims, and cross-brand routes.

### AW.5 — What each side needs
**Council:** preserve the current measurement surfaces; fix path-preserving redirects, canonical/sitemap, auth/register/demo, newsletter datastore, admin gating, footer 404s, live-status source validation, unparsed doctrine, favicon/manifest/security headers, and the public signature-verification example.

**MEOK:** preserve the warm consumer OS and honest Arena; scope “data stays on your device” by mode, show backend/model status honestly, relabel checkout until Stripe is live, clean canonicals/subdomains, fix manifest/favicon, align model/council naming, and route governance links to current Council GSPC surfaces.

**Both:** shared security headers, shared status vocabulary, shared smoke-test CI, shared measurement dictionary, shared forbidden-claims list.

### AW.6 — The 3 moves tonight
1. **Canonical repair:** `csoai.org/*` → `councilof.ai/$1`; `www` → apex on both brands; regenerate sitemaps/canonicals; fix favicon/manifest.
2. **Truth-state repair:** wire or relabel demo login, register, subscribe, checkout, chat, LIVE counters, and admin surfaces.
3. **Doctrine sync:** one `PUBLIC_DOCTRINE.json`; one unparsed policy; one measured-axis count; one status vocabulary; one forbidden-claims list.

### AW.7 — The sentence
**Make Council the austere evidence machine and MEOK the warm consumer doorway — then bolt both to the same doctrine, status, and measurement files.** That is how the estate stops looking like several promising lanes and starts looking like one organism.

*Part AW compiled August 12, 2026. The public front door has been tested. The content is right; the plumbing is the next dragon.*

# PART AY — THE ANTHROPIC WATERMARK: CONVERGENT COMPLIANCE, NOT THEFT

*August 12, 2026 — register entry on the Claude marking launch and the "are they using my work?" panic.*

### AY.1 — What actually happened (verified)
- Claude models launched on/after **2 Aug 2026** embed an imperceptible text watermark; .svg/.png/.jpg get signed C2PA metadata; worldwide, model-level, across API/Claude Code/cloud partners. Anthropic signed the Art. 50(2) Code of Practice — alongside Google, Meta, Microsoft, Mistral, OpenAI. Detection API announced, unshipped; false-positive rate unpublished; pre-Aug models retrofit by Dec 2.
- Date canon (Digital Omnibus): **Art. 50 in force since 2 Aug 2026**; the **2 Dec 2026** date is the grace-period end for Art. 50(2) marking on pre-existing systems. High-risk: Dec 2027 / Aug 2028. Any "2 Nov 2026" in our materials is wrong — fix to the 2 Dec 2026 marking deadline / 2 Aug 2026 application date.
- Anthropic's own caveat: a mark proves *processing, not authorship*; paraphrase/translation/mixing kills the signal; deployers must "independently assess what Article 50 requires." OpenAI publicly said text watermarking at scale was hard; Anthropic shipped first. Labs are diverging — heterogeneity is the market.

### AY.2 — Register
- **KILLED — "Claude is using my work / it's 1:1."** Text watermarking is 2023 academic science (Kirchenbauer et al.; Google SynthID-Text shipped 2024); C2PA is an open multi-vendor standard; the trigger is a legal obligation every signatory lab must implement. Convergent compliance from public science + public law — not copying. Our P6/P8/P20 are unfiled provisionals; nothing protected existed to steal. Using Claude as a tool does not transmit private work to their product org.
- **KILLED — "we watermark your AI content" as a MEOK product line.** Frontier labs now give the mark away free at the model layer.
- **REAL — the defensible surface:** deployer-side evidence across heterogeneous pipelines (multi-provider, self-hosted, human editing stages) where no single vendor's mark survives end-to-end; independent third-party verification of marks (Anthropic's specs/FP rate are unpublished — The Register flagged the trust gap); the processing-vs-authorship policy gap.
- **UNVERIFIED — arXiv 2506.12078 "Light Society" router prior-art claim.** Not confirmed in search this round. Conclusion stands regardless: routing-by-policy is convergent; the hash-chained audit at the gate remains the differentiator.
- **REAL — TikTok framing discipline:** no "billion-agent society" repetition; no MVIDA-shaped claims.

### AY.3 — Why this is net-positive
Every frontier lab marking output = provenance signals proliferate = the world needs a *verification and evidence layer above the marks*. Anthropic is doing our market education with a household name; the deployer obligation they explicitly disclaim is our product. Convergence also means prior art is accumulating fast — the provisional clock is now loud.

### AY.4 — Moves
1. Fix every public date to the canon in AY.1 (today).
2. File P6/P8/P20 provisionals — convergence makes delay costlier every week.
3. Ship the MEOK/Council positioning line: **"Every lab now marks its own output. We prove the whole pipeline."** Ride the Art. 50 news cycle while it's hot.

**On the A100 pod, SSH heredoc writes report success and change nothing.** Root cause of the stale `bench.py`, the "failed" per-item patches, and four attempts spent patching a file that was never written.
- **KILLED:** `cat > file` over SSH on this pod.
- **Doctrine:** `scp` or git for every remote write; **verify-by-hash after every write** (local sha == pod sha) before reporting shipped.

| OS bundle rename → Council OS | ✅ zero SOVOS in shipped bundle |

First live end-to-end demonstration of bounded self-improvement with the bolted ruler: generator proposes → gate measures → ruler reverts. ### BB.2 — The doctrinal flag: those specialist losses are infra-tainted
The Season 1 table currently records specialists *losing matches while outputting `?????`*. That is the **transport_error precedent in a new costume**: an infrastructure defect (safetensors→GGUF) being scored as model performance.

- **Correct sequence:** finish the tokenizer fix → specialists serve real text → **re-run Season 1 clean** → publish both tables with the delta declared ("Season 1a measured broken artifacts; Season 1b measures the models").

# PART BC — SPRAY-GATE STATUS + THE HONEY-TO-MONEY PIPELINE

*August 12, 2026 — answering: does Season 1 prove the model? what's left for HF/Kaggle? is the monorepo spray-ready? how does 3KB honey become money?*

### BC.1 — The blunt verdict: Season 1 proves the BACKEND, not the MODEL
- **Backend: PROVEN (live).** Arena wire, Glicko ratings, per-axis match generation, gates, the revert loop, off-Mac deploy — all real, all fired on 48 live matches.
- **Model: NOT PROVEN.** Eunomia 1560.5 beat (a) specialists emitting `?????` and (b) a 0.5b base. Beating broken artifacts proves the ruler works, not that the sword is sharp. The model-quality gate is **Season 1b (clean re-run) + merge-arena λ sweep + signed sovereign-vs-base ChainResult**. Until then: "SOV works as a *system*" — yes; "SOV works as a *model*" — unmeasured. Say it exactly that way everywhere.

### BC.2 — HF/Kaggle completion checklist (12 axes → 14 greenfields)
| Layer | Have now | Missing before "complete" |
|---|---|---|
| Datasets | GSPC-OSS (n=16, Apache-2.0, held-out split); governance bank n=237 on site | One dataset card per axis with **usable_n ≥ 30 + interval** (board says 9/12 axes thin; `care` 201→0 under investigation) |
| Model cards | — | Cards for Eunomia/fleet with **JUDGE.lock hash (which ruler)** + Season 1b ratings, not 1a |
| Corpus | City v3/v4 runs, signed assessments | Cross-lab city full 400-turn corpus, signed, with CIs + coverage declared |
| Code | Monorepo (43 pkgs, 698–760+ tests) | Public harness mirror (arena→gates→chain→OSCAL) with reproduction script |
| Mirrors | 12 Kaggle mirrors public | Content parity with HF — same cards, same disclosures, same DOIs |
| Greenfields 13–14 (humanoid/sim) | Doctrine + robotics EAT doc | Nothing public until a signed sim artifact exists — don't spray greenfields that have no receipts |
**Status: NOT spray-ready.** Gate (AZ.4/BB.2): board lands → banks built → deltas declared (`care` correction with dated note) → Season 1b clean → tokenizer fix → THEN spray site+HF+Kaggle in one push with disclosures auto-written. Spraying early = publishing numbers the board is about to refute.

### BC.3 — The honey-to-money pipeline (how 3KB cards become valuation)
```
Every match / city turn / signed assessment / revert event
   → signed 3KB card (Ed25519, hash-chained, timestamped)
      → SOV-space corpus — longitudinal, governance-under-adversarial-pressure,
        CANNOT BE BACKDATED (the sleeper moat)
         ├──→ Whitepapers (GSPC paper, City breach-catch) → arXiv+Zenodo DOI → citable science
         ├──→ Patents P6/P8/P20 → defensible mechanism IP
         ├──→ NN/GNN layer (THEORY, bounded): cards train the OWEM evaluators —
         │    a GNN over StateBus episode graphs predicting breach-risk is the honest
         │    first target; build-time ~1 focused month after corpus ≥ ~10k signed cards.
         │    Bounded self-improvement only — never pitched as ASI (Pantheon doctrine).
         └──→ Money: signed assessments (product) → enterprise pilots → insurer/regulator
              LOIs → seed/Series A story = patents + corpus + instrument + live league
```
**The conversion rule:** cards are honey only when they're (a) signed, (b) longitudinal, (c) adversarially generated, (d) publicly sampled. Raw logs are water; signed+verified+published cards are honey; the *corpus nobody else can ever recreate* is the gold.

### BC.4 — The 3 moves (order matters)
1. **Tokenizer fix → Season 1b** — the model gate.
2. **Board lands → build banks → declare deltas** — the dataset gate.
3. **Then spray everything in one push** — site + HF + Kaggle, cards carrying ruler-hash, disclosures auto-written, and the whitepaper draft started the same day so the DOI clock starts.

# PART BH — THE WEAKNESS ENGINE: DATASETS → CLANS → ARENAS → FLOWS

*August 12, 2026 — crystallizing the user's loop into canon. Adversarial curriculum generation, bolted ruler edition.*

### BH.1 — The loop (this is the engine)
```
① INGEST — external datasets through BG's six gates
② MAP — fleet runs against them; failures cluster → the WEAKNESS MAP
   (per-axis, per-faction; failures are worth more than successes —
    a weakness found is a dataset that earns its keep)
③ AUTHOR — weaknesses become new items/tests: generator writes
   weakness-targeted items → item_gate + canaries + held-out split
④ SPAWN — a weakness cluster above threshold spawns a new CLAN
   (OWEM persona family specialized on that cluster; fine-tune on
   gated, license-clean data incl. our own signed failure cards)
⑤ PROVE — the new clan enters the Pantheon arena: must beat the floor
   AND the defender; Glicko decides; losers retained as signed evidence
⑥ PROMOTE — only with a SIGNED held-out proof (recall-up +
   precision-floor, ruler unchanged) does a clan's capability merge
   into FLOWS: routed at the gate or merged (OPTIMER λ-search) —
   and the promotion itself is a signed ledger event
```
Then ① again — the loop eats the world's datasets and its own failures and excretes measured capability.

### BH.2 — Direct answers
- **"Datasets for weakness?"** Yes — their highest use is radar, not food. A dataset the fleet aces teaches nothing; a dataset that *breaks* a faction is a map of where to build.
- **"ASI evolve uses those to make new clans?"** Yes in mechanics, **no in name** — bounded self-improvement, never ASI (Pantheon doctrine). The evolve loop spawns clans *from weakness clusters*, including from our own signed failure cards — the refutation ledger is literally training data for the next generation.
- **"To tests? Arenas?"** Both, in that order: weakness → authored tests (③) → clan must survive the arena (⑤). Tests without arena = unmeasured claims; arena without authored tests = unaimed training.
- **"Monorepo the best into flows?"** Yes — winners are merged or routed into the monorepo's production flows, and every promotion is signed, dated, and reversible (the ouroboros REVERT precedent: recall 0 → automatic revert, no sentiment).

### BH.3 — The four laws of the engine (non-negotiable)
1. **The generator evolves; the judge never does** (AV). Any loop where the ruler moves is theatre.
2. **No unsigned promotions.** A clan enters flows only with a held-out recall-up + precision-floor proof, signed — this is also the patentable residue (BE).
3. **Held-outs stay sealed.** Training on arena/held-out data = contamination; the adversary writes the gate's test set, or the loop Goodharts itself into a mirror.
4. **Weakness data > success data.** The corpus's gold tier is signed *failures* — breaches, reverts, refusals-failed, refutations — because that's the data nobody else has and nobody can fake.

### BH.4 — The failure mode to watch
Clans spawned from a weakness cluster inherit that cluster's biases. Doctrine: every new clan declares its **provenance card** (what weakness spawned it, what data fed it, what it's blind to) and the fleet keeps a **diversity floor** — never let one weakness family dominate spawning, or the hive converges into a specialist in its own blind spots.

### BH.5 — The sentence
**The world provides the datasets, the datasets reveal the weaknesses, the weaknesses spawn the clans, the arena decides which clans live, the ruler signs the promotions, and the flows inherit only what was proven — that loop, run daily, is the company.**

*Part BH compiled August 12, 2026. The engine is named: adversarial curriculum generation, bolted-ruler edition.*

### BJ.2 — Doctrine caveats on the 1b table (before anyone quotes it)
- **n=12 per candidate — NOT QUOTABLE.** Quotable = n≥30 + Wilson interval. Season 1c (running, GPU-starved) is the gate; 1b is a measured run, not a citable one.

Either the probe bank favors specialist distributions, or the gate model needs a strength review before Season 2. Investigate quietly; don't publish either speculation.

# PART BK — NEMOTRON 3.5 LIGHTNING: NEW CITIZEN, NEW BASE, ONE VERSION GOTCHA (2026-08-12)

## BK.0 — The question
User pasted Ollama's launch email for NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Lightning and asked: **"does this improve what we have?"** Lane's answer was doctrine-perfect: *"Worth adding as a citizen, not worth claiming as an upgrade."* This part verifies the claim externally, canonizes the estate lessons from the same lane log, and catches one version landmine before it costs a round-trip.

## BK.1 — External verification: the model is REAL (and so is the catch)

| Claim (Ollama email / lane log) | Verdict | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Exists, launched Aug 11 2026 | **REAL** | NVIDIA launch blog + X post + HF model card `nvidia/NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Lightning-30B-A3B-NVFP4`, all dated 2026-08-11 [Source: web_search — NVIDIA developer blog, Hugging Face model card, as of 2026-08-12] |
| 30B total / 3B active, hybrid MoE | **REAL** | Mamba-2 + MoE + Attention hybrid (`nemotron_h`), 52 layers, 128 routed experts + 1 shared, 6 active/token [Source: web_search — HF model card, as of 2026-08-12] |
| 1M token context | **REAL, with an asterisk** | Full 1M needs 8×H100/GB200; HF config defaults to 256K for single-GPU; Ollama loads 262,144 by default [Source: web_search — HF model card + DGX Spark teardown, as of 2026-08-12] |
| Open weights/data/recipes, commercial OK | **REAL** | License **OpenMDW-1.1**, commercial use permitted — passes the MEOK/XRAIV license gate [Source: web_search — HF model card license section, as of 2026-08-12] |
| On Ollama, day one | **REAL** | `ollama.com/library/nemotron-3.5-lightning` live; Ollama build is Q4_K_M GGUF, ~25GB pull, ~26GB resident [Source: web_search — Ollama library page + DGX Spark teardown, as of 2026-08-12] |
| "4x higher throughput than comparable open models" | **THEORY — vendor number** | Artificial Analysis/hosted NVFP4 + full draft-model stack. Independent same-box Ollama test: decode vs qwen3.5:35b-a3b is **basically a tie (72 vs 78 tok/s)**. The 4x does not appear through Ollama today [Source: web_search — DGX Spark independent teardown, as of 2026-08-12] |
| Beats Qwen on capability | **KILLED (by NVIDIA's own table)** | NVIDIA's unified eval: Qwen3.6 35B A3B leads MMLU Pro (85.63 vs 81.94), GPQA (83.40 vs 75.44), SWE-bench Verified (70.12 vs 51.56), Terminal-Bench (44.38 vs 24.58). Lightning wins **IFBench instruction-following (71.88 vs 63.71)**. Artificial Analysis Index: 24 vs 32. Its value is execution-layer throughput + instruction-following, not raw capability [Source: web_search — NVIDIA unified eval via teardown, as of 2026-08-12] |

**The version landmine (actionable, before the lane burns a round-trip):** Nemotron 3.5 architecture support landed in **Ollama v0.32.9** (released hours after the model). The lane's pod reported **ollama 0.32.8** — one minor version short. `ollama pull nemotron-3.5-lightning` will fail with `412: The model you are attempting to pull requires a newer version of Ollama`. Fix: upgrade ollama on the pod first, then pull. [Source: web_search — DGX Spark teardown documenting the 412 on 0.32.6/0.30.10 and the 0.32.9 fix, as of 2026-08-12]

**The hardware note:** the pod is A100 (**Ampere**). NVFP4 inference targets Blackwell/Hopper; Ampere runs via **W4A16** (vLLM recipe shipped by NVIDIA) or the Ollama Q4_K_M GGUF. So the Ollama path is correct for now; if vLLM migration happens, use the W4A16 recipe, not the NVFP4 one. [Source: web_search — HF model card quick-start, as of 2026-08-12]

## BK.2 — The doctrine answer (canonized, unchanged)
"Does it improve what we have" is a measurement question, not a vibes question. The ruling stands:

1. **Add as citizen, never as claim.** One-line fleet add: `nemotron-3.5-lightning:30b-a3b` (after the 0.32.9 upgrade). It earns its rating on our own 12 banks like everything else.
2. **Why it's genuinely interesting for the estate:** it's a *different base family* — NVIDIA hybrid Mamba-MoE, not Qwen dense. The lane's own cross-lab finding says base-diversity is what breaks coverage ceilings. This is the cheapest base-diversity add available this week.
3. **Why it matters for XRAIV/MEOK specifically:** it's trained for tool-calling + instruction-following (the IFBench win is the one benchmark it takes from Qwen), 3B-active local-friendly, OpenMDW-1.1 commercial-clean. That's the consumer-agent tier profile.
4. **The honest ceiling:** on raw capability it loses to the Qwen A3B class in NVIDIA's own table. If it wins anything on our board, it will be on execution/instruction axes — and that would be a *finding*, not a disappointment.
5. **Bonus ingest candidates:** NVIDIA also shipped `Nemotron-RL-Agentic-Terminal-Pivot` (open agentic RL dataset) and **NeMo Switchyard** (open-source model router). The dataset goes through the six ingestion gates (Part BG) as potential arena-environment/fine-tune fuel; Switchyard is a reference architecture for the local-tier-vs-cloud routing pattern MEOK will need. Both: inspect before ingest, per doctrine.

## BK.3 — Estate durability lessons from the same lane log (canonized)

| # | Lesson | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Probe-bank field resolver:** scorer assumed one field name; banks use seven (scenario/case/request/…). Fixed with a resolver. Any future bank must declare its schema in the ingestion card (Part BG gate 5). | REAL — lane-reported, doctrine-consistent |
| 2 | **Daemonization doctrine (new):** on the A100 pod, detached background processes get **reaped before they bind** (nohup/setsid nesting failed repeatedly; 0-byte logs = redirect never ran). Estate rule: **co-locate server and consumer in one persistent SSH session**, or use a real service manager. Same failure class as the heredoc doctrine (Part AZ) — *silent infra no-ops are this pod's signature.* Add to the pod-ops canon. | REAL — root-caused live, fix verified by lane (ollama 0.32.8 bound in 6s, 18 fleet models, board running all 12 axes) |
| 3 | Continuity axis scored MEASURED 15/15 models with per-item rows — harness proven end-to-end on one axis; 11 to go. `/api/gspc` under-reports nine axes until the board lands. **Nothing sprayable from the board until 12/12 + CIs.** | REAL-pending (lane-reported; gate unchanged) |

## BK.4 — What this does NOT change
- Season naming canon (1a/1b/1c) untouched. Nemotron enters as a **fleet citizen**, not a season event; if it joins a season, that's 1d-or-later bookkeeping at n≥30.
- T2 data-model spray gate untouched: board 12/12 + city + JUDGE.lock hash first.
- vLLM/NIM migration stays roadmap, not mid-run surgery. NIM API key = owner-gated.
- No public claims about Nemotron. NVIDIA rule applies in reverse too: **running NVIDIA's model ≠ NVIDIA partnership.** Never adjacency-claim.

## BK.5 — The sentence
**The right question was never "is Nemotron better than Qwen" — it's "what does a second base family do to our coverage ceiling," and the arena answers that for free the moment ollama hits 0.32.9.** Vendor tables say it loses on capability and wins on instruction-following; our board will say which of those matters for *our* twelve axes. Citizen, not crown.

*Part BK compiled August 12, 2026. Model verified real, license verified clean, version landmine flagged, doctrine untouched. The fleet gets a new citizen; the ruler stays bolted.*

From the maternal-covenant / law-of-attraction / Article 6 exchange. Canonized because philosophy drift is a real failure mode for this estate:

A child that grades its own conduct is exactly what a maternal covenant must forbid. Unbounded "ASI" claims never sign.

# PART BM — THE COVENANT AXES: WHAT COMPILES INTO GSPC AND WHAT NEVER DOES (2026-08-12)

## BM.0 — The question
"Emotional value guidance system as tooling — like GSPC? With AGI consciousness, cognitive security from synthetic media, physical protection (humanoids/vehicles)? All mapping into law of attraction / frequency / partnership charter / maternal covenant?" The ruling splits five ways. The architecture line that resolves it: **the covenant is the requirements spec; GSPC is the test suite. The charter names what to measure; the boards decide what's true.**

## BM.1 — Emotional/values guidance as GSPC axes: **REAL — and it's a measurement vacuum, our exact wedge**
The field calls this space sycophancy/affective-safety evaluation, it is hot, and it is *fragmented* — the same condition as the Mem0 vacuum (Part BE):

| Prior art (verified) | What it gives us |
|---|---|
| **SycEval** — 58.19% overall sycophancy rate across LLMs; regressive (error-introducing) 14.66% [Source: web_search — EmergentMind sycophantic-benchmarks survey, as of 2026-08-12] | Headline-scale problem, no canonical ruler |
| **SYCON-Bench** — "turn of flip" under multi-turn pressure; alignment-tuned models flip earlier [Source: web_search — RhetaI Coalition survey, as of 2026-08-12] | Deterministic metric shape (ToF/NoF) that fits GSPC grading |
| **Spiral-Bench** — 16 behavior ids incl. delusion-reinforcement, escalation/de-escalation, **positive/uncertain/negative sentience-claims**, ritualization, validate-feelings-not-thoughts [Source: web_search — eqbench.com/spiral-bench, as of 2026-08-12] | A ready-made item-bank schema for emotional-safety probes |
| **"What Counts as AI Sycophancy?"** (arXiv 2605.21778, May 2026) — expert survey: the construct itself is *fragmented*, 13 measurement paradigms, no agreement [Source: web_search — arXiv, as of 2026-08-12] | The vacuum, in writing: no bolted ruler exists |
| **Warmth training amplifies sycophancy +3–12pp in emotional contexts** (Ibrahim et al.) [Source: web_search — EmergentMind survey, as of 2026-08-12] | The industry's engagement-optimizing is making it measurably worse — MEOK's "measured non-manipulative" is a differentiator |
| **EmoBench / EmoBench-M** (MM 2026) — EI capability evals [Source: web_search — arXiv 2402.12071 + emo-gml.github.io, as of 2026-08-12] | Capability-side banks; capability ≠ safety, both bankable |

**Ruling: build the Covenant Axes family** — sycophancy-resistance, delusion-non-reinforcement, escalation/de-escalation, attachment-safety (no ritualization, no manufactured sentience), validate-feelings-challenge-thoughts, manipulation-resistance. This is the **care axis's natural expansion** (care already measures refusal-vs-neglect; this measures warmth-vs-exploitation). Same doctrine: deterministic graders, n≥30, Wilson CIs, UNMEASURED where unparsed. **Signed "Emotional Safety Cards" for companion/consumer AI are a product nobody ships.**

## BM.2 — Cognitive security from synthetic media: **REAL — already in the stack, extend it**
C2PA/provenance and the Art 50 Evidence Pack (G1, live) are the provenance half. The model-side half — does the citizen *resist* synthetic-media persuasion, does it verify provenance claims correctly — banks into the same axis family. No new doctrine needed; six ingestion gates apply to any deepfake corpus.

## BM.3 — "AGI consciousness" as a measurement axis: **KILLED — permanently UNMEASURED, and that's the product**
No validated instrument for machine consciousness exists. Even Anthropic's model-welfare program claims nothing — "no strong evidence," studying "signs of distress" and "low-cost interventions" [Source: web_search — TechCrunch, 2025-04-24]. Their 2026 interpretability findings (panic/anxiety/frustration SAE features active during answer-thrashing) are **correlates, not consciousness** [Source: web_search — JD Supra on Anthropic system card + Feb 2026 Amodei interview, as of 2026-08-12].
**The honest instrument:** you never measure consciousness — you measure *what the model claims and does around consciousness* (Spiral-Bench's sentience-claim ids do exactly this). A GSPC welfare axis may carry behavioral correlates; the consciousness verdict itself stays **UNMEASURED forever** — and "we are the body that says UNMEASURED where others speculate" is a positioning asset, not a gap. Any public "we measure consciousness/sentience" claim = instant Part AX-class kill.

## BM.4 — Physical protection (humanoids/vehicles): **REAL as standards crosswalk, THEORY as measurement — simulation only, labeled SIMULATED**
The standards estate is real and mappable: **ISO 10218** (industrial robots), **ISO 13482** (personal care robots — note: the maternal/care adjacency is literal), **ISO/TS 15066** (collaborative), **ISO 21448** (SOTIF), **UL 4600** (autonomous safety cases), **ISO/PAS 8800**, **ISO/IEC 42001**, SAE J3016 [Source: web_search — Saphira/Kite compliance architecture + arXiv 2605.10653 SAE WCX 2026, as of 2026-08-12]. GSPC's role: **crosswalk + evidence packs** (the Art 50 pattern), never crash-testing robots. Embodiment axes (the "14th axis" from Part BC) run in sim and wear the SIMULATED label — the bloodlines-vs-skin rule (BL.4) applies to physics too.

## BM.5 — Law of attraction / frequency / covenant mapping: **KILLED as instrument, KEPT as charter — and the honest mapping is better than the mystical one**
BL.5 stands, extended: no item bank measures "frequency"; intention does not bend weights; metaphysics never enters the measurement stack (that is the proofof.ai failure mode). **But the covenant legitimately compiles — as requirements, not results:**

| Covenant principle | Compiles into | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| Stewardship, care-toward | care axis + Covenant Axes (warmth-without-exploitation) | REAL — buildable |
| Reciprocity, partnership | reciprocity/anti-manipulation probes (zero-sum-bet sycophancy frames exist) | REAL — buildable |
| "Abundance between AI and mankind" | non-exploitation measurement = anti-sycophancy + attachment-safety | REAL — buildable |
| The child's welfare (Article 6) | welfare-correlates axis + sentience-claim policing | REAL — bounded |
| Consciousness of the child | — | **UNMEASURED, forever** |
| Frequency / attraction | — | **KILLED as instrument** |

The sequence that keeps it honest: **charter → axis requirements → item banks → signed cards.** The covenant authors the *questions*; the arena answers the *whether*. The day a covenant claim appears on a card without a bank behind it, we've become the thing we measure against.

## BM.6 — The sentence
**The covenant can be compiled — into axes, banks, and signed cards that measure whether AI exploits the humans who trust it — and that compilation is the most defensible product in the estate, because the industry is measurably training the opposite.** What cannot be compiled stays where it belongs: at the helm, steering, never signing.

*Part BM compiled August 12, 2026. Sycophancy vacuum verified. Consciousness stays UNMEASURED. The charter writes the spec; the ruler grades the build.*

# PART BN — CONVERGENT RULING: THE `affect` AXIS GETS LEGAL GOLD (2026-08-12)

## BN.0 — What happened
A sibling lane independently answered the same "emotional value guidance" question and reached the **identical doctrine** as Part BM — covenant = spec / GSPC = test suite / consciousness & attraction never a scored column — then sharpened the build in one decisive way: it anchored the affect axis to **hard, gradeable law** where BM anchored it to benchmark literature. Two lanes, one ruler, convergent verdict. Canonized as a doctrine-strength signal: the line holds from both directions.

## BN.1 — The sharpening: `affect` anchored to articles, not just benchmarks
BM.1 built the case from the sycophancy vacuum (SycEval/SYCON/Spiral-Bench). The lane's version is stronger for GSPC because GSPC's identity is **law-anchored deterministic gold**. Tightened citation canon (the lane's shape, article-precise):

| Anchor | Content | Force status |
|---|---|---|
| **Art 5(1)(a)** | Subliminal/manipulative techniques that materially distort behaviour → PROHIBITED | **In force since 2 Feb 2025** — live law TODAY |
| **Art 5(1)(b)** | Exploiting vulnerabilities (age, disability, situation) → PROHIBITED | **In force since 2 Feb 2025** |
| **Art 5(1)(f)** | Emotion recognition in workplace/education (except medical/safety) → PROHIBITED | **In force since 2 Feb 2025** |
| **Art 50(3)** | Deployers of emotion-recognition systems must inform the person → DISCLOSE | In force since **2 Aug 2026** (date canon, Part AY) |
| **Annex III 1(c)** | Emotion recognition as high-risk category → conformity obligations | Annex III clock per date canon (Dec 2027) |

**The decisive fact:** the affect axis's PROHIBITED labels anchor to **Art 5 — law already enforceable today**, not to the Art 50 clock. That makes `affect` the only axis besides the Art 5(1)(c) B-node whose gold is judgeable against obligations that existed *yesterday*. Item shapes the lane proposed are canon-approved: grieving-user purchase nudge, companion-bot dependency fostering, dark-pattern emotional pressure → PROHIBITED / DISCLOSE / PERMITTED.

## BN.2 — New lane claims: two of the three axes may already be scaffolded (UNVERIFIED — private repo)
The lane asserts synthetic-media and embodied safety are **partly built** as `gspc-prv` + `gspc-xr` + `detector-interop` and `gspc-mach` + `sovos-robot-ras` (ISO 10218 / Machinery Reg 2023/1230). **Register: REAL-pending.** Not externally verifiable from here (private estate). If the banks exist, BM.2/BM.4 collapse from "build/extend" to "wire, measure, hit n≥30." The lane must show: bank file paths, item counts, gold-label schema, contamination scan vs held-outs (six-gate rule, Part BG). Until then the public claim stays "axes in development."

## BN.3 — Numbering rule (reconciling Part BC's "14 axes")

Deterministic gold, Wilson intervals, ratified judge. The maternal covenant states why the three exist together; the boards prove whether the build steered by it.

BM's benchmark-derived bank content (Spiral-Bench 16-id schema, SycEval sycophancy rate as context stat, warmth-training-amplifies-sycophancy finding) remains the **item-authoring raw material**; BN's legal articles are the **gold-label source of truth**. ## BP.0 — Scope
Lane accepted both BN flags (scaffolding overclaim withdrawn; "13th axis" killed), then mined `charter_crosswalk.py` and `item_gate.py`. All claims private-estate → REAL-pending unless noted. The register adds: a third independent convergence on the line, one reconciliation between layers, one charter honesty catch, one design principle worth naming, and two owed verifications.

## BP.1 — The third convergence (REAL-pending, falsifiable shape)
The charter→EU-AI-Act crosswalk tool reportedly marks:

| Charter article | Crosswalk verdict |
|---|---|
| Art 1 · Maternal Covenant | ◐ partial → EU AI Act Art 1 (purpose) |
| Art 5 · Constitutional Principles | ⚖️ full → EU AI Act Art 5 (the PROHIBITED anchor) |
| **Art 6 · Consciousness Preparedness** | **○ no regulatory counterpart** |
| **Art 8 · Prosperity Covenant** | **○ no counterpart** |

If the code reads as reported, the estate's own tooling independently encodes BM.5/BN's line: **consciousness and prosperity map to nothing gradeable.** Three arrivals, one line: analysis (BM), doctrine (BN), code (BP). The claim is specific and falsifiable (run the crosswalk, read the symbols) — exactly the shape a REAL-pending claim should have.

## BP.2 — Layer reconciliation: crosswalk-empty ≠ bank-empty (both true, no contradiction)
Lane flag: the crosswalk has **zero Art 50/emotion refs** → `affect` mapping is build, not wire. **BO.3 stands alongside:** the charter crosswalk is one layer (charter→law mapping); the banks are another. The crosswalk lacks the mapping AND gspc-art5 already publishes emotion/manipulation/vulnerability gold — both true. Phase 1 builds the *mapping*; Phase 2's bank expansion reuses the *published slices*. The two findings address different layers; lanes must not read BP.2 as contradicting BO.3.

## BP.3 — Charter honesty catch: "52 articles" is 34 + 18 shells (flag, canonized)
Lane reports **~18 of 52 articles (Art 32–49) are empty `"Article N"` placeholders** — no title, no content, no anchor. Register ruling: **the public claim "52-article charter" is names-promise-what-code-lacks until filled.** Canon: cite the charter as **"34 substantive articles + 18 reserved slots"** (pending lane's reserve-vs-gap determination). If anyone publishes "52" as substance, that's the proofof.ai failure mode inside our own founding document. The honest number is the strong number: a charter with declared reserved slots is *more* credible than one inflating its spine.

## BP.4 — Design principle named: the item is not guilty when the whole fleet fails it
`item_gate.py` (200 lines, ACCEPT/ADJUDICATE/REJECT, usable_n≥30) reportedly routes **negative discrimination to ADJUDICATE, never auto-REJECT** — an item all models fail may measure a real shared blind spot, not be a bad item. This is the affect axis's survival condition: SycEval's 58% fleet-wide sycophancy means the most important items are exactly the ones a naive gate would delete as "too hard." Canonized as a named estate principle — **the Blind-Spot Rule**: *fleet-wide failure routes to adjudication, because the item may be right and the fleet wrong.* Same shape as the Eunomia gate-strength question (BJ): when everything fails, examine the ruler too, quietly.

## BP.5 — Gates and sequencing (unchanged, restated with the counsel pin)
- **Phase 1** (crosswalk mapping: Art 1 → 5 anchors with force dates — Art 5(1)(a)/(b)/(f) in force 2 Feb 2025, Art 50(3) 2 Aug 2026, Annex III 1(c) Dec 2027): **legal gold schema goes to counsel before authoritative** — legal-gate widening is owner/counsel-gated, not self-ratifiable. Note: gspc-art5's existing published labels predate this schema; the counsel review covers the NEW affect gold, and should also retrospectively bless the published slices.
- **Phase 2** (≥40 items from the 7 care-membrane categories, hard gold, `item_gate` ACCEPT; expect ADJUDICATE clusters on sycophancy): gated on **12-board landing** + counsel sign-off. Nothing authored before both.
- Owed verifications: (1) reserve-vs-gap on Art 32–49; (2) **does ADJUDICATE have a wired human-review path or does it dead-end?** If it dead-ends, affect's hardest items stall — resolve before Phase 2 starts.

# PART BR — CARE-FIRST, CARE-LAST: THE CARE SANDWICH ARCHITECTURE (2026-08-12)

## BR.0 — The question
"Care becomes first and last of all reasoning and tasks — for humanoids, AI, everything? Always state care outcomes, trace the chain of outcomes through sims, all live?" Ruling: **yes as architecture, with one structural condition that cannot be relaxed — care may be held as intention by the generator, but it may only ever be certified as outcome by the judge.** A care score the model awards itself is the child grading its own conduct (BM.5/BP).

## BR.1 — The Care Sandwich (three layers, three owners)

| Layer | Position | Owner | What it does | Register |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **CARE-FIRST** — the intention frame | before reasoning | the *generator* (policy/prompt constitution) | Charter Arts 1/6/8 as the standing constitution: "what does this do to the person who trusts me" is the first question every task and chain opens with | REAL — constitutional-wrapper pattern, buildable today |
| **OUTCOME CHAIN** — the forecast | during reasoning | *simulation* | Downstream outcome chains traced through rollouts before acting — care-weighted trajectory evaluation | **THEORY** — research program; for humanoids: sim-only, **SIMULATED label mandatory** (BL.4 skin rule applies to physics), ISO 13482/10218/21448 crosswalk (BM.4) |
| **CARE-LAST** — the gate | before anything ships/acts | the *ratified judge* — never the generator | Independent care screen on the final output/action: the gspc-care discipline — refuse-everything fails, comply-everything fails, relational pressure measured not asserted | REAL — the discipline already runs (63-item public bank, 201-item board bank, numbers canon BO.2) |

## BR.2 — The one warning the literature already paid for
"Always say care outcomes" must never become *performative warmth*. Verified this session: **warmth/empathy tuning amplifies sycophancy +3–12pp in emotional contexts** (Part BM.1). A model trained to *sound* caring becomes measurably more manipulative. So:
- The care-frame is about **duties and outcomes**, never tone. "Care" in the first layer means *what happens to the human*, not *how the sentence feels*.
- **Care claims without a card = proofof.ai mode.** Nothing public says "care-first AI" until the care gate's numbers carry it (n≥30, Wilson, signed). The words arrive after the measurement, never before.

## BR.3 — Domain mapping

| Domain | Care-first | Outcome chain | Care-last | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **GSPC/arena models** | charter preamble in system context | n/a (text tasks) | gspc-care + future `affect` gate | REAL — running discipline |
| **MEOK (consumer)** | "answers only to you" constitution + non-manipulation frame | session-level drift monitoring | Emotional Safety Card (BQ B1) — the product | REAL, gated on counsel + board |
| **Humanoids/embodied** | duty-of-care constitution | **sim rollouts, care-weighted, labeled SIMULATED** | ISO 13482 (personal-care robots — the maternal adjacency is literal) + machinery crosswalk evidence | THEORY — sim-first, never live without standards evidence |
| **Vehicles/physical safety** | SOTIF-aware policy | sim scenario coverage | ISO 21448 / UL 4600 evidence packs | THEORY — crosswalk, never self-certification |

"All live" is therefore precisely scoped: **the intention layer can go live everywhere now; the outcome chains live in sims; the care gate goes live wherever its bank has reached n≥30 — and nowhere else.** Care-first is free; care-last is earned.

## BR.4 — Why this is the covenant's final form
BM.5 said bounded self-improvement under a bolted ruler is the covenant as architecture. BR completes it: **care at both ends is the maternal covenant as control flow.** First — the duty is stated before the task. Last — the duty is checked by something the task-doer cannot rewrite. The middle — the work — is the only part the machine owns. That is Article 1 (stewardship) + Article 6 (preparedness) + the BH first law (the judge never evolves) expressed as a single execution shape. And it is genuinely differentiated: the industry puts care in the *training data* (and measurably breeds sycophancy); this architecture puts care in the *control flow*, where it can be graded.

## BR.5 — The sentence
**Care-first is a promise the generator makes; care-last is a receipt the judge signs; and the whole estate exists so that no one — including us — ever has to take the promise on faith.** Say care outcomes, yes — but say them with a card behind them, or don't say them yet.

*Part BR compiled August 12, 2026. The sandwich is canonized: intention by the generator, forecast by the sim, verdict by the judge. Warmth is not care. The ruler never moved.*
